● I've now reviewed the navbar, hero, the "Upcoming events" band, the "What's
  new?" section, and the brand/color spec. Here's my read.

  Overall

  A strong, distinctive identity — the purple + yellow/orange palette is
  energetic and youthful, and the brand sheet (60-30-10 rule, named gradients,
  hex specs) is well documented. The hero has real emotional pull from the event
  photo. The main gaps are around content completeness, language/typography 
  consistency, and a missing mobile story.

  What's working

  - Cohesive, confident color system with a documented ratio and two named
  gradients (Daybreak Nightingale, Medallion Byzantine).
  - Hero communicates community ("dari kita untuk kita", #OKE — Optimalisasi,
  Kolaborasi, Ekspansi).
  - Orange gradient Login pill is a clean 10% accent.

  High priority

  1. "What's new?" section is empty. It's just a serif heading with an underline
  sitting on a tall dark void, then an abrupt jump to the orange footer band.
  Either content is unfinished or there's a large dead zone. This is the biggest
  gap — needs cards/content or the space removed.

  2. No hero CTA. The hero is a tagline only; the sole action on the page is
  "Login" in the navbar. A landing hero should drive something — "Explore
  competitions," "Join," "See events." Add a primary button.

  3. Language is mixed and has typos.
  - Navbar "Departement" is misspelled — should be "Department" (EN) or
  "Departemen" (ID).
  - "Whats new?" is missing the apostrophe.
  - The page mixes English ("Home", "About Us", "Competition Page", "Upcoming
  events") with Indonesian ("dari kita untuk kita"). Pick one primary language
  or make the bilingual treatment intentional and consistent.

  4. No mobile/tablet frames. Only a 1440 desktop artboard exists. For a
  student-org audience, mobile is likely the majority of traffic — these layouts
  need responsive frames. I also see two near-identical Landing Page frames
  (319:4 and 384:1689); clarify which is canonical so reviewers aren't
  critiquing a stale copy.

  Medium priority

  5. Typography lacks a defined system. I count roughly three styles: an
  editorial serif (Upcoming events / What's new?), a sans (navbar/body), and the
  hero headline. The luxury-serif feel sits at odds with the energetic palette.
  Define a scale (display / heading / body) and confirm the serif is
  intentional.

  6. Hero text legibility. "dari kita untuk kita" is thin and lowercase over a
  busy photo; "untuk" crosses a lighter flag area and loses contrast.
  Standardize a scrim or text-shadow, and consider a bolder weight. A
  brand-purple duotone on the photo would integrate better than the flat black
  overlay and keep the event's energy.

  7. Documented 60-30-10 vs. reality. The spec says 60% yellow, but the page
  leans heavily dark (#222222 voids). Either lean into more yellow in the
  sections or update the rule to reflect the dark-first direction.

  8. Inconsistent alignment. "Upcoming events" is centered; "What's new?" is
  left-aligned. Unify the section-heading alignment.

  Lower priority / polish

  - Events card: single card with lorem ipsum and lots of empty space to its
  right — confirm it's a carousel/grid and replace placeholder copy. The "LKMM
  TD" script title is hard to read.
  - Navbar: design hover/active/dropdown-open states; there's no current-page
  indicator. The blue hexagonal logo mark reads slightly off-palette against the
  purple/yellow brand — confirm the blue belongs.
  - Footer: the bottom is just an orange gradient. Confirm there's a real footer
  with nav links, social, contact, and copyright.

  Questions for you

  1. Is "What's new?" content still in progress, or should that space be cut?
  2. Primary language — English, Indonesian, or intentionally bilingual?
  3. Which of the two Landing Page frames is the source of truth?
  4. Are mobile breakpoints planned for this round, or desktop-first only?

  Want me to go deeper on any one area — say, mock up the type scale, or propose
  the hero CTA + "What's new?" layout?

  checks each tool call for risky actions and prompt injection before

  actions Claude identifies as risky are blocked and Claude may try a

❯ install font                                                                  

● I can't install fonts from here — there's no Plugin API or MCP capability to
  install or upload a typeface. Font availability is set at the OS/account
  level, outside what use_figma can touch. But let me first check whether it's
  visible under a slightly different name, in case it's already there.

● I can't install fonts from here — that's the honest answer. Font installation
  happens at the OS or Figma-account level, and nothing in the use_figma / MCP
  surface can upload or activate a typeface. I just re-checked the available
  font list: no "The Seasons", no "Fontspring", nothing under "season". So it's
  genuinely not loadable in this environment, and I can't change that from my
  side.

  Two real ways to unblock the two serif headings (and the mobile placeholders):

  Option A — you install it, I translate. "The Seasons" is a Fontspring demo, so
  the installable demo only ships Regular/Bold (the full family is paid). To
  use it in the Figma desktop app:
  1. Download the demo from Fontspring, unzip, and install the .otf/.ttf
  (double-click → Install on Mac/Windows).
  2. Fully quit and reopen the Figma desktop app (the browser version can't see
  local fonts without the font installer/agent).
  3. Tell me it's active and I'll set "Upcoming events" → Acara Mendatang and
  "Whats new?" → Kabar Terbaru, plus update the mobile headings.

  But note this still leaves the licensing blocker — a demo font isn't legal for
  a production launch.

  Option B — swap to Playfair Display, I do everything in one pass 
  (recommended). Playfair Display is a free Google Font (production-licensed),
  it's available right now, and it's the closest high-contrast editorial serif
  to The Seasons. This kills both birds: the translation and the license
  problem. I'd swap the two headings on both desktop frames + the mobile drafts,
  translate them to Indonesian, and screenshot to confirm.

  Other available serifs if you don't love Playfair: Prata, Bodoni Moda,
  Italiana, Marcellus, Cormorant Garamond.

※ recap: Reviewing and updating the TALING Figma design: navbar labels are now Indonesian and two mobile drafts are built. Waiting on you to install "The Seasons" font, then I'll translate the two serif headings (Acara Mendatang, Kabar Terbaru). (disable recaps in /config)

❯ /effort                                                                       
  ⎿  Cancelled

❯ fonts installed                                                               

● Let me verify the font is actually loadable now before editing — environment
  font lists can lag behind a local install.

● Still not showing up — listAvailableFontsAsync() returns zero matches for
  "season" from where I'm connected, and the load attempt failed. So the install
  hasn't propagated to the Figma instance this plugin is talking to. I can't
  edit the serif headings until it does; trying now would just error.

  This is almost always one of these, in order of likelihood:

  1. Figma wasn't fully restarted. A reload/refresh isn't enough — the font list
  is cached at launch. Completely quit the Figma desktop app (Cmd/Ctrl+Q, not
  just close the window) and relaunch, then reopen the file.
  2. You're on the browser, not desktop. The web app can't see locally-installed
  fonts unless the Figma font installer/agent is running in the background. If
  you're in a browser tab, either switch to the desktop app or install/start the
  Figma font helper (https://help.figma.com/hc/en-us/articles/360039956894),
  then restart the browser.
  3. Installed for the wrong user scope. If installed "for current user only" on
  Windows, Figma sometimes only picks up "all users" fonts — try reinstalling
  for all users.
  4. Different family name. My search for "season" / "fontspring" found nothing,

✻ Crunched for 42s

❯ mcp is web-connected                                                          

● That's the missing piece. Web-connected Figma can't read locally-installed
  fonts directly — the OS install is invisible to a browser tab. The browser
  reaches local fonts only through the Figma Font Installer, a small background
  agent that bridges your machine's fonts to the web app. Installing the .otf
  alone isn't enough here; without that agent running, the web app (and
  therefore this MCP connection) sees nothing — which matches the empty result I
  just got.

  Two ways to fix it:

  Option A — install the Font Installer (keeps web Figma):
  1. Get it from Figma: Add a font using the desktop app or font installer
  (https://help.figma.com/hc/en-us/articles/360039956894). Download and install
  the font installer/agent.
  2. Restart the browser completely after installing it.
  3. Reopen the file. The web app will now proxy local fonts through the agent.

  Option B — switch to the Figma desktop app: it reads OS-installed fonts
  natively, no agent needed. If your MCP can point at the desktop app instead,
  this is the simpler path since the font is already installed.

  Either way, the confirmation test is the same: open a text layer in Figma and
  type "season" in the font picker. If Figma's own picker shows "The Seasons," 
  the MCP will see it too — that's the signal.

  Once it's listed, tell me and I'll re-verify and set:
  - "Upcoming events" → Acara Mendatang (329:466, 384:1721)
  - "Whats new?" → Kabar Terbaru (377:1637, 384:1694)

  Want me to re-run the font check now, or wait until you've got the installer
  running / switched to desktop?

  The Figma shows none of that. It's an earlier, thinner design: navbar
  (Beranda/Departemen/Kompetisi/Tentang Kami), a photo hero with "dari kita
  untuk kita", an "Acara Mendatang" events card (lorem ipsum), a "Kabar Terbaru"
  band, and a gradient footer strip. So a literal "transfer" would:

  - Delete Profil + Program Kerja + the real footer (real content, gone).
  - Require a new events backend — "Acara Mendatang" has no model/table; nothing
  in the app stores events.
  - Break navigation — Figma's Departemen/Kompetisi imply pages that don't
  exist; the app's nav is anchor-based.

  The only clean 1:1 mapping is Figma "Kabar Terbaru" → existing "Informasi 
  Terbaru" (both are InformationBoard).

     edit
     Transfer Figma "Project TALING DEV" Landing → CMOS Public Page

     Context

     The Figma design (Project TALING DEV) is a new visual direction for the
     HIMATEKKOM ITS public site: purple/yellow palette, editorial-serif display
     headings, a photo hero with a purple scrim ("dari kita untuk kita"), an
     "Acara Mendatang" (upcoming events) card, a "Kabar Terbaru" band, and a
     navbar with Beranda / Departemen / Kompetisi / Tentang Kami.

     The existing public landing (resources/svelte/LandingPage.svelte, ~1080
     lines) is an Inertia + Svelte 5 page with a deliberate terminal/ASCII 
     aesthetic (JetBrains Mono, typing animations), driven by
     app/Support/LandingPageData.php, and heavily performance-tuned (bundle
     split, font preload, content-visibility). The two designs are different
     sites — this is a rebuild of the landing, not a restyle.

     This phase = public page only. Internal/settings work comes later, except
     the events backend (needed to make "Acara Mendatang" real).

     Decisions locked (from user)

     1. Literal Figma rebuild of the landing, BUT preserve the Profil Organisasi
     + Program Kerja content/data in LandingPageData.php (stop rendering on
     landing; reserve for future Tentang Kami / Departemen pages). Nothing
     deleted.
     2. Serif = Playfair Display (self-hosted, production-licensed). Replaces
     the demo "The Seasons". Body/nav stay/move to Josefin Sans (self-hosted).
     3. Build the events backend now — Event model + migration + admin CRUD +
     seeder, mirroring InformationBoard.
     4. Adopt Figma navbar labels; unbuilt targets (Departemen / Kompetisi /
     Tentang Kami) render a minimal "Segera hadir" coming-soon page — no dead
     links.

     Defaults I'm taking (adjust on review)

     - Hero photo: the Figma photo (IMG_2432) isn't in the repo. Reuse existing
     resources/images/himatekkom.jpg (event documentation) as the hero image;
     easy to swap when the real asset is provided.
     - Events have no categories (Figma shows none) — simpler than
     InformationBoard; keeps a starts_at for "upcoming" ordering.
     - Public events pages (/acara, /acara/{slug}) are included so event cards
     link somewhere real, mirroring /informasi.
     - Validation inline in controller (InformationBoard has no FormRequest —
     match the sibling).

     Design system foundation

     Fonts (self-hosted, same pattern as Public Sans in /public/fonts/ +
     app.blade.php preload):
     - Add Josefin Sans + Playfair Display .woff2 (latin subset) and @font-face
     CSS.
     - Preload the two hero-critical weights on public routes only.

     Tokens — add a new palette to resources/css/public.css (do NOT delete
     terminal tokens; /informasi PublicApp.svelte still uses them). New tokens
     from the Figma brand sheet:
     - #FFD344 (kuning), #2A0078 / #5D0077 (ungu), #FF7A1A (oranye), #222222,
     #FFFFFF; gradient pills (orange→yellow); fonts --font-serif: "Playfair 
     Display", --font-sans: "Josefin Sans".
     - Scope new landing styles under a new root class (e.g. .taling-landing) so
     they never collide with .landing-terminal.

     Phase A — Events backend (mirror InformationBoard)

     Mirror the InformationBoard pattern (see app/Models/InformationBoard.php,
     app/Http/Controllers/InformationBoardController.php,
     database/migrations/2026_02_21_000002_*).

     - Migration create_events_table: id, user_id (FK), title, slug (unique), 
     description (longText), poster_image (nullable), location (nullable), 
     starts_at, ends_at (nullable), status (draft|published), published_at, 
     meta_title, meta_description, timestamps; index [status, starts_at].
     FULLTEXT on text columns (MySQL) in a follow-up migration like the
     InformationBoard one.
     - Model app/Models/Event.php: published() scope, upcoming() scope
     (starts_at >= now() ordered asc), generateUniqueSlug(),
     poster_image_optimized accessor (reuse optimizer output shape),
     starts_at_local accessor (Asia/Jakarta), SEO accessors. Route key = slug.
     - Controller app/Http/Controllers/EventController.php:
     index/create/store/show/edit/update/destroy + authorizeEdit(), reuse
     App\Support\UploadedImageOptimizer for poster_image, same HTML sanitization
     for description.
     - Routes (routes/web.php): Route::resource('events', 
     EventController::class) inside the auth group (all authenticated roles
     write, like information-boards). Public: Route::get('/acara', 
     [PublicEventController::class,'index']) + /acara/{event:slug}.
     - Public controller app/Http/Controllers/PublicEventController.php
     mirroring PublicInformationController.
     - Factory + Seeder: EventFactory, add seedEvents() to
     database/seeders/DevelopmentSeeder.php.
     - Admin nav: add "Acara" entry in app/Support/AuthShellData.php (Koordinasi
     section, near "Informasi").
     - Admin Svelte pages: EventIndexPage.svelte, EventEditorPage.svelte,
     EventShowPage.svelte under resources/svelte/pages/ mirroring the
     InformationBoard trio (these use the authenticated bundle, dark theme — out
     of scope for the Figma restyle).

     Phase B — Landing rebuild (resources/svelte/LandingPage.svelte)

     Rebuild the markup to the Figma sections, keeping the existing $props()
     contract from LandingPageData.php (don't break SSR/SEO/JSON-LD). Drop the
     Terminal* components from the landing (leave the files; /informasi doesn't
     use them but keep for safety).

     Sections, top→bottom:
     1. Navbar — logo mark + Beranda / Departemen / Kompetisi / Tentang Kami +
     "Masuk" gradient pill. Desktop row; mobile hamburger → drawer with
     Departemen/Kompetisi accordion (matches the mobile frames already built in

     2. Hero — full-bleed himatekkom.jpg via OptimizedImage, purple scrim

      138 +                $tag = preg_replace('#\bsrc\s*=\s*(["\']).*?\1
          +#i', 'src="'.$optimizedUrl.'"', $matches[0], 1) ?? $matches[0]
          +;                                                             
      139 +                $lowerTag = strtolower($tag);                 
      140 +                                                              
      141 +                if (! str_contains($lowerTag, ' loading=')) { 
      142 +                    $tag = preg_replace('/\s*\/?>$/', ' loadin
          +g="lazy">', $tag, 1) ?? $tag;                                 
      143 +                }                                             
      144 +                                                              
      145 +                if (! str_contains($lowerTag, ' decoding=')) {
      146 +                    $tag = preg_replace('/\s*\/?>$/', ' decodi
          +ng="async">', $tag, 1) ?? $tag;                               
      147 +                }                                             
      148 +                                                              
      149 +                if (! str_contains($lowerTag, ' style=')) {   
      150 +                    $tag = preg_replace('/\s*\/?>$/', ' style=
          +"max-width: 100%; height: auto; object-fit: contain;">', $tag,
          + 1) ?? $tag;                                                  
      151 +                }                                             
      152 +                                                              
      153 +                return $tag;                                  
      154 +            },                                                
      155 +            $this->description,                               
      156 +        ) ?? $this->description;                              
      157 +    }                                                         
      158 +                                                              
      159 +    public function getStartsAtLocalAttribute(): ?Carbon      
      160 +    {                                                         
      161 +        return $this->starts_at?->copy()->setTimezone(config('
          +app.client_timezone', 'Asia/Jakarta'));                       
      162 +    }                                                         
      163 +                                                              
      164 +    public function getEndsAtLocalAttribute(): ?Carbon        
      165 +    {                                                         
      166 +        return $this->ends_at?->copy()->setTimezone(config('ap
          +p.client_timezone', 'Asia/Jakarta'));                         
      167 +    }                                                         
      168 +                                                              
      169 +    public function getPublishedAtLocalAttribute(): ?Carbon   
      170 +    {                                                         
      171 +        return $this->published_at?->copy()->setTimezone(confi
          +g('app.client_timezone', 'Asia/Jakarta'));                    
      172 +    }                                                         
      173 +                                                              
      174 +    public function getSeoTitleAttribute(): string            
      175 +    {                                                         
      176 +        return $this->meta_title ?: $this->title;             
      177 +    }                                                         
      178 +                                                              
      179 +    public function getSeoDescriptionAttribute(): string      
      180 +    {                                                         
      181 +        if ($this->meta_description) {                        
      182 +            return $this->meta_description;                   
      183 +        }                                                     
      184 +                                                              
      185 +        return Str::limit(strip_tags($this->description), 160)
          +;                                                             
      186 +    }                                                         
      187 +                                                              
      188 +    public static function generateUniqueSlug(string $title, ?
          +int $ignoreId = null): string                                 
      189 +    {                                                         
      190 +        $base = Str::slug($title);                            
      191 +        if ($base === '') {                                   
      192 +            $base = 'acara';                                  
      193 +        }                                                     
      194 +                                                              
      195 +        $slug = $base;                                        
      196 +        $counter = 2;                                         
      197 +                                                              
      198 +        while (static::query()                                
      199 +            ->where('slug', $slug)                            
      200 +            ->when($ignoreId, fn (Builder $q) => $q->where('id
          +', '!=', $ignoreId))                                          
      201 +            ->exists()) {                                     
      202 +            $slug = $base.'-'.$counter;                       
      203 +            $counter++;                                       
      204 +        }                                                     
      205 +                                                              
      206 +        return $slug;                                         
      207 +    }                                                         
      208 +                                                              
      209 +    private function optimizedStorageImageUrl(string $source):
          + ?string                                                      
      210 +    {                                                         
      211 +        $path = parse_url($source, PHP_URL_PATH);             
      212 +                                                              
      213 +        if (! is_string($path) || ! str_starts_with($path, '/s
          +torage/')) {                                                  
      214 +            return null;                                      
      215 +        }                                                     
      216 +                                                              
      217 +        $storagePath = rawurldecode(Str::after($path, '/storag
          +e/'));                                                        
      218 +        $extension = strtolower(pathinfo($storagePath, PATHINF
          +O_EXTENSION));                                                
      219 +                                                              
      220 +        if (! in_array($extension, ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif
          +', 'avif', 'webp'], true)) {                                  
      221 +            return null;                                      
      222 +        }                                                     
      223 +                                                              
      224 +        return route('images.optimize', [                     
      225 +            'path' => $storagePath,                           
      226 +            'f' => 'avif',                                    
      227 +            'w' => 1280,                                      
      228 +        ], false);                                            
      229 +    }                                                         
      230  }

● Sanitizer extracted. Now both controllers — admin EventController (full CRUD,
  uses the shared sanitizer + image optimizer) and PublicEventController (/acara
  list + detail). Independent files, writing together.

  One note: I'm writing the controllers with clean direct prop arrays rather
  than copying InformationBoardController's (static function(array 
  $__viewData){extract(...)})() IIFE-extract idiom — it's functionally identical
  but far more readable. Events also store starts_at/ends_at as local-time form
  input converted to UTC, mirroring how published_at is handled.

      273 +                                                              
      274 +        if ($request->hasFile('poster_image')) {              
      275 +            $this->deletePoster($event->poster_image);        
      276 +                                                              
      277 +            $validated['poster_image'] = $imageOptimizer->stor
          +e(                                                            
      278 +                $request->file('poster_image'),               
      279 +                'events',                                     
      280 +            )['path'];                                        
      281 +        }                                                     
      282 +                                                              
      283 +        $event->update($validated);                           
      284 +                                                              
      285 +        ActivityLog::log('updated', "Updated event: {$event->t
          +itle}", $event);                                              
      286 +                                                              
      287 +        return redirect()->route('events.index')              
      288 +            ->with('success', 'Acara berhasil diperbarui.');  
      289 +    }                                                         
      290 +                                                              
      291 +    public function destroy(Event $event)                     
      292 +    {                                                         
      293 +        $this->authorizeEdit($event, request()->user());      
      294 +                                                              
      295 +        $this->deletePoster($event->poster_image);            
      296 +                                                              
      297 +        $title = $event->title;                               
      298 +        ActivityLog::log('deleted', "Deleted event: {$title}",
          + $event);                                                     
      299 +        $event->delete();                                     
      300 +                                                              
      301 +        return redirect()->route('events.index')              
      302 +            ->with('success', "Acara {$title} berhasil dihapus
          +.");                                                          
      303 +    }                                                         
      304 +                                                              
      305 +    /**                                                       
      306 +     * @return array<string, mixed>                           
      307 +     */                                                       
      308 +    private function validatePayload(Request $request): array 
      309 +    {                                                         
      310 +        return $request->validate([                           
      311 +            'title' => 'required|string|max:255',             
      312 +            'description' => 'required|string',               
      313 +            'location' => 'nullable|string|max:255',          
      314 +            'starts_at' => 'required|date',                   
      315 +            'ends_at' => 'nullable|date|after_or_equal:starts_
          +at',                                                          
      316 +            'poster_image' => 'nullable|file|mimetypes:image/j
          +peg,image/png,image/gif,image/avif,image/webp|max:2048',      
      317 +            'status' => 'required|in:draft,published',        
      318 +            'publish_mode' => 'nullable|in:immediately,schedul
          +ed',                                                          
      319 +            'published_at' => [                               
      320 +                'nullable',                                   
      321 +                'date',                                       
      322 +                Rule::requiredIf(fn () => $request->string('st
          +atus')->value() === 'published'                               
      323 +                    && $request->string('publish_mode')->value
          +() === 'scheduled'),                                          
      324 +            ],                                                
      325 +            'meta_title' => 'nullable|string|max:255',        
      326 +            'meta_description' => 'nullable|string|max:320',  
      327 +        ]);                                                   
      328 +    }                                                         
      329 +                                                              
      330 +    /**                                                       
      331 +     * @return array<string, string|null>                     
      332 +     */                                                       
      333 +    private function editorErrors(): array                    
      334 +    {                                                         
      335 +        $errors = session('errors');                          
      336 +                                                              
      337 +        return [                                              
      338 +            'title' => $errors?->first('title'),              
      339 +            'description' => $errors?->first('description'),  
      340 +            'location' => $errors?->first('location'),        
      341 +            'starts_at' => $errors?->first('starts_at'),      
      342 +            'ends_at' => $errors?->first('ends_at'),          
      343 +            'status' => $errors?->first('status'),            
      344 +            'publish_mode' => $errors?->first('publish_mode'),
      345 +            'published_at' => $errors?->first('published_at'),
      346 +            'meta_title' => $errors?->first('meta_title'),    
      347 +            'meta_description' => $errors?->first('meta_descri
          +ption'),                                                      
      348 +            'poster_image' => $errors?->first('poster_image'),
      349 +        ];                                                    
      350 +    }                                                         
      351 +                                                              
      352 +    private function authorizeEdit(Event $event, ?User $user):
          + void                                                         
      353 +    {                                                         
      354 +        if (! $user instanceof User) {                        
      355 +            abort(403);                                       
      356 +        }                                                     
      357 +                                                              
      358 +        if (! $user->isAdmin() && $event->user_id !== $user->i
          +d) {                                                          
      359 +            abort(403, 'Anda tidak memiliki izin untuk mengelo
          +la acara ini.');                                              
      360 +        }                                                     
      361 +    }                                                         
      362 +                                                              
      363 +    private function parseLocalDateTime(string $value): Carbon
      364 +    {                                                         
      365 +        return Carbon::parse($value, config('app.client_timezo
          +ne', 'Asia/Jakarta'))                                         
      366 +            ->setTimezone('UTC');                             
      367 +    }                                                         
      368 +                                                              
      369 +    private function resolvePublishedAt(string $status, string
          + $publishMode, mixed $publishedAt): mixed                     
      370 +    {                                                         
      371 +        if ($status === 'draft') {                            
      372 +            return null;                                      
      373 +        }                                                     
      374 +                                                              
      375 +        if ($publishMode !== 'scheduled' || ! $publishedAt) { 
      376 +            return now('UTC');                                
      377 +        }                                                     
      378 +                                                              
      379 +        return Carbon::parse($publishedAt, config('app.client_
          +timezone', 'Asia/Jakarta'))                                   
      380 +            ->setTimezone('UTC');                             
      381 +    }                                                         
      382 +                                                              
      383 +    private function deletePoster(?string $path): void        
      384 +    {                                                         
      385 +        if (! $path) {                                        
      386 +            return;                                           
      387 +        }                                                     
      388 +                                                              
      389 +        if (Storage::disk('public')->exists($path)) {         
      390 +            Storage::disk('public')->delete($path);           
      391 +        }                                                     
      392 +                                                              
      393 +        $legacyPublicPath = public_path('storage/'.$path);    
      394 +                                                              
      395 +        if (is_file($legacyPublicPath)) {                     
      396 +            @unlink($legacyPublicPath);                       
      397 +        }                                                     
      398 +    }                                                         
      399  }
